/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 557-603
Documentation:

Method code:
557 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
558 |       switch (n.getType()) {
559 |         case Token.NULL:
560 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
561 |           break;
562 | 
563 |         case Token.VOID:
564 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
565 |           break;
566 | 
567 |         case Token.STRING:
568 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
569 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
570 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
571 |           }
572 |           break;
573 | 
574 |         case Token.NUMBER:
575 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
576 |           break;
577 | 
578 |         case Token.TRUE:
579 |         case Token.FALSE:
580 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
581 |           break;
582 | 
583 |         case Token.REGEXP:
584 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
585 |           break;
586 | 
587 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
588 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
589 |           if (info != null &&
590 |               info.getLendsName() != null) {
591 |             if (lentObjectLiterals == null) {
592 |               lentObjectLiterals = Lists.newArrayList();
593 |             }
594 |             lentObjectLiterals.add(n);
595 |           } else {
596 |             defineObjectLiteral(n);
597 |           }
598 |           break;
599 | 
600 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): If we ever support Array tuples,
601 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
602 |       }
603 |     }

===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1947-1987
Documentation:

Method code:
1947 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1948 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
1949 |         return;
1950 |       }
1951 | 
1952 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
1953 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
1954 |       }
1955 | 
1956 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 2) {
1957 |         // We only need to worry about escaped variables at depth 3.
1958 |         // An variable escaped at depth 2 is, by definition, a global variable.
1959 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
1960 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
1961 |         return;
1962 |       }
1963 | 
1964 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
1965 |         String name = n.getString();
1966 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
1967 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
1968 |         if (var != null) {
1969 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
1970 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
1971 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
1972 |           }
1973 |         }
1974 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
1975 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
1976 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
1977 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
1978 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
1979 |         if (var != null) {
1980 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
1981 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
1982 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
1983 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
1984 |           }
1985 |         }
1986 |       }
1987 |     }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionType.java
Original range: 313-332
Documentation:
/**
   * Two union types are equal if they have the same number of alternates
   * and all alternates are equal.
   */
Method code:
313 |   @Override
314 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType object) {
315 |     if (object == null) {
316 |       return false;
317 |     }
318 |     if (object.isUnionType()) {
319 |       UnionType that = object.toMaybeUnionType();
320 |       if (alternates.size() != that.alternates.size()) {
321 |         return false;
322 |       }
323 |       for (JSType alternate : that.alternates) {
324 |         if (!hasAlternate(alternate)) {
325 |           return false;
326 |         }
327 |       }
328 |       return true;
329 |     } else {
330 |       return false;
331 |     }
332 |   }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1262-1310
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1262 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1263 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1264 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1265 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1266 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1267 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1268 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1269 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1270 |       } else if (info != null) {
1271 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1272 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1273 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1274 |           } else {
1275 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1276 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1277 |           }
1278 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1279 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1280 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1281 |         } else {
1282 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1283 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1284 |             JSType knownType = null;
1285 |             if (rValue != null) {
1286 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1287 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1288 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1289 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1290 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1291 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1292 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1293 |                 // reasons.
1294 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1295 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1296 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1297 |                     && lValue.isName()
1298 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1299 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1300 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1301 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1302 |                 }
1303 |               }
1304 |             }
1305 |           }
1306 |         }
1307 |       }
1308 | 
1309 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1310 |     }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2021-2033
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as synactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2021 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2022 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2023 |         n.isGetElem());
2024 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2025 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2026 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2027 |         || parent.isVar()
2028 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2029 |         || parent.isDec()
2030 |         || parent.isInc()
2031 |         || parent.isParamList()
2032 |         || parent.isCatch();
2033 |   }

===== M0043 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0060 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1982-2021
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
1982 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
1983 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
1984 |     if (alternate != null) {
1985 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
1986 |     }
1987 | 
1988 |     Node expr = null;
1989 |     do {
1990 |       if (expr != null) {
1991 |         skipEOLs();
1992 |         token = next();
1993 |         Preconditions.checkState(
1994 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
1995 | 
1996 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;
1997 |         if (isPipe && match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1998 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatiblity.
1999 |           next();
2000 |         }
2001 |         skipEOLs();
2002 |         token = next();
2003 |       }
2004 |       expr = parseTypeExpression(token);
2005 |       if (expr == null) {
2006 |         return null;
2007 |       }
2008 | 
2009 |       union.addChildToBack(expr);
2010 |       // We support commas for backwards compatiblity.
2011 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));
2012 | 
2013 |     if (alternate == null) {
2014 |       skipEOLs();
2015 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2016 |         return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
2017 |       }
2018 |       next();
2019 |     }
2020 |     return union;
2021 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 646-705
Documentation:

Method code:
646 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
647 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
648 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(type);
649 | 
650 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null; name = name.getNext()) {
651 |       scope = traverse(name.getFirstChild(), scope);
652 |     }
653 | 
654 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types, or changed with
655 |     // type casts.
656 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
657 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
658 |     // TODO(nicksantos): There should be an "anonymous object" type that
659 |     // we can check for here.
660 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
661 |     if (objectType == null) {
662 |       return scope;
663 |     }
664 | 
665 |     boolean hasLendsName = n.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
666 |         n.getJSDocInfo().getLendsName() != null;
667 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName() && !hasLendsName) {
668 |       return scope;
669 |     }
670 | 
671 |     String qObjName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(
672 |         NodeUtil.getBestLValue(n));
673 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
674 |          name = name.getNext()) {
675 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
676 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(name);
677 |       if (memberName != null) {
678 |         JSType rawValueType =  name.getFirstChild().getJSType();
679 |         JSType valueType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
680 |             name, rawValueType);
681 |         if (valueType == null) {
682 |           valueType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
683 |         }
684 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, valueType, name);
685 | 
686 |         // Do normal flow inference if this is a direct property assignment.
687 |         if (qObjName != null && name.isString()) {
688 |           String qKeyName = qObjName + "." + memberName;
689 |           Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(qKeyName);
690 |           JSType oldType = var == null ? null : var.getType();
691 |           if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
692 |             var.setType(oldType == null ?
693 |                 valueType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(oldType));
694 |           }
695 | 
696 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(name, qKeyName,
697 |               oldType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : oldType,
698 |               valueType);
699 |         }
700 |       } else {
701 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
702 |       }
703 |     }
704 |     return scope;
705 |   }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 423-448
Documentation:

Method code:
423 |   @Override
424 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
425 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
426 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
427 |     switch (n.getType()) {
428 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
429 |         // normal type checking
430 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
431 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
432 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
433 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
434 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
435 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
436 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
437 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
438 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
439 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
440 |         }
441 | 
442 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
443 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
444 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
445 |         break;
446 |     }
447 |     return true;
448 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 399-412
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
399 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
400 |       Node ret;
401 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
402 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
403 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
404 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       } else {
407 |         ret = transform(n);
408 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
409 |       }
410 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
411 |       return ret;
412 |     }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 498-555
Documentation:

Method code:
498 |     @Override
499 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
501 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
502 | 
503 |       switch (n.getType()) {
504 |         case Token.CALL:
505 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
506 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
507 |           break;
508 | 
509 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
510 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
511 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
512 |           }
513 | 
514 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
515 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
516 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 | 
520 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
521 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
522 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
523 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
524 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
525 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
526 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
527 |           }
528 |           break;
529 | 
530 |         case Token.CATCH:
531 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.VAR:
535 |           defineVar(n, parent);
536 |           break;
537 | 
538 |         case Token.GETPROP:
539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
541 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
542 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 |       }
546 | 
547 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
549 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
550 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
551 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
552 |         }
553 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
554 |       }
555 |     }

===== M0084 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.matchConstraint(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 554-580
Documentation:

Method code:
554 |   @Override
555 |   public void matchConstraint(ObjectType constraintObj) {
556 |     // We only want to match contraints on anonymous types.
557 | 
558 |     // Handle the case where the constraint object is a record type.
559 |     //
560 |     // param constraintObj {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
561 |     // function f(constraintObj) {}
562 |     // f({});
563 |     //
564 |     // We want to modify the object literal to match the constraint, by
565 |     // taking any each property on the record and trying to match
566 |     // properties on this object.
567 |     if (constraintObj.isRecordType()) {
568 |       for (String prop : constraintObj.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
569 |         JSType propType = constraintObj.getPropertyType(prop);
570 |         if (!isPropertyTypeDeclared(prop)) {
571 |           JSType typeToInfer = propType;
572 |           if (!hasProperty(prop)) {
573 |             typeToInfer = getNativeType(JSTypeNative.VOID_TYPE)
574 |                 .getLeastSupertype(propType);
575 |           }
576 |           defineInferredProperty(prop, typeToInfer, null);
577 |         }
578 |       }
579 |     }
580 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 778-804
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infimums, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
778 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
779 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
780 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
781 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
782 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
783 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
784 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
785 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
786 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
787 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
788 |           needsFiltering = true;
789 |           break;
790 |         }
791 |       }
792 | 
793 |       if (needsFiltering) {
794 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
795 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
796 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
797 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
798 |           }
799 |         }
800 |         return builder.build();
801 |       }
802 |     }
803 |     return type;
804 |   }

   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.

94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 191-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
191 |   @Override
192 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
193 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
194 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
195 |     // show up in the type registry.
196 |     Scope newScope = null;
197 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
198 |     if (parent == null) {
199 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
200 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
201 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
202 | 
203 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
204 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
205 | 
206 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
207 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
208 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
209 |     } else {
210 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
211 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
212 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
213 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
218 | 
219 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
220 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
221 |     // build properties on.
222 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
223 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
224 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
225 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
226 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
227 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
229 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
230 |         }
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     if (parent == null) {
235 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
236 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
237 |           delegateCallingConventions);
238 |     }
239 |     return newScope;
240 |   }

===== M0098 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1559-1611
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1559 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1560 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1561 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1562 | 
1563 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1564 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1565 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1566 |       return;
1567 |     }
1568 | 
1569 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1570 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1571 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1572 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1573 | 
1574 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1575 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1576 |       if( functionJSDocInfo != null  &&
1577 |           functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode() != null) {
1578 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1579 |       }
1580 | 
1581 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1582 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1583 |       // in an extern.
1584 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1585 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1586 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1587 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1588 |            !isExtern)) {
1589 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1590 |       }
1591 | 
1592 |       // Functions with explcit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1593 |       // or GETELEM.
1594 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1595 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1596 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isNativeObjectType() &&
1597 |           !(child.isGetElem() ||
1598 |             child.isGetProp())) {
1599 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1600 |       }
1601 | 
1602 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1603 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1604 |     } else {
1605 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1606 |     }
1607 | 
1608 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1609 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1610 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1611 |   }

/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
925 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
926 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
927 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
928 |     }
929 | 
930 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
931 | 
932 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
933 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
934 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
935 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
936 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
937 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);

939 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
940 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
941 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
942 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
943 |         } else {
944 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
945 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
946 |         }
947 |       }

Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
683 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
684 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
685 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
686 |       return that.toMaybeUnionType().getLeastSupertype(this);
687 |     }
688 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
689 |   }

===== M0118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 557-587
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
557 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
558 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
559 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
560 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
561 |     if (isOptional) {
562 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
563 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
564 |       // error can be reported.
565 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
566 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
567 |         emittedWarning = true;
568 |       }
569 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
570 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
571 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
572 |         emittedWarning = true;
573 |       }
574 |     } else {
575 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
576 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
577 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
578 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
579 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
580 |         } else {
581 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
582 |         }
583 |         emittedWarning = true;
584 |       }
585 |     }
586 |     return emittedWarning;
587 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1641-1671
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1641 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1642 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1643 |         Node n = stub.node;
1644 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1645 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1646 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1647 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1648 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1649 | 
1650 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1651 |           continue;
1652 |         }
1653 | 
1654 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1655 |         // in the type registry.
1656 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1657 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1658 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1659 | 
1660 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1661 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1662 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1663 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1664 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1665 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1666 |         } else {
1667 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1668 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1669 |         }
1670 |       }
1671 |     }

===== M0146 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1714-1740
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1714 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1715 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1716 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1717 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1718 |       skipEOLs();
1719 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1720 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1721 |       skipEOLs();
1722 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1723 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1724 |       skipEOLs();
1725 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1726 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1727 |       String string = stream.getString();
1728 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1729 |         skipEOLs();
1730 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1731 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1732 |         return newStringNode(string);
1733 |       } else {
1734 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1735 |       }
1736 |     }
1737 | 
1738 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1739 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1740 |   }

203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

162 |   @Override
163 |   public JSType findPropertyType(String propertyName) {
164 |     JSType propertyType = null;
165 | 
166 |     for (JSType alternate : getAlternates()) {
167 |       // Filter out the null/undefined type.
168 |       if (alternate.isNullType() || alternate.isVoidType()) {
169 |         continue;
170 |       }
171 |

173 |       if (altPropertyType == null) {
174 |         continue;
175 |       }
176 | 
177 |       if (propertyType == null) {
178 |         propertyType = altPropertyType;
179 |       } else {

181 |       }
182 |     }
183 |

484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1667-1708
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1667 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1668 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1669 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1670 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1671 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1672 |       // {?} - right curly
1673 |       // {?=} - equals
1674 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1675 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1676 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1677 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1678 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1679 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1680 |       token = next();
1681 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1682 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1683 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1684 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1685 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1686 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1687 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1688 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1689 |       }
1690 | 
1691 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1692 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1693 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1694 |     } else {
1695 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1696 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1697 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1698 |           next();
1699 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1700 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1701 |           next();
1702 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1703 |         }
1704 |       }
1705 | 
1706 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1707 |     }
1708 |   }

   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.

386 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,

/**
   * FieldTypeList := FieldType | FieldType ',' FieldTypeList
   */
Method code:
2089 |   private Node parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken token) {
2090 |     Node fieldTypeList = newNode(Token.LB);
2091 | 
2092 |     do {
2093 |       Node fieldType = parseFieldType(token);
2094 | 
2095 |       if (fieldType == null) {
2096 |         return null;
2097 |       }
2098 | 
2099 |       fieldTypeList.addChildToBack(fieldType);
2100 | 
2101 |       skipEOLs();
2102 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
2103 |         break;
2104 |       }
2105 | 
2106 |       // Move to the comma token.
2107 |       next();
2108 | 
2109 |       // Move to the token passed the comma.
2110 |       skipEOLs();
2111 |       token = next();
2112 |     } while (true);
2113 |

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1220 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1224 |       }
1225 |     }
1226 |     return builder.build();
1227 |   }

Original range: 3032-3051
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3032 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3033 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3034 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3035 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3036 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3037 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3038 |       return parent;
3039 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3040 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3041 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3042 |       return parent;
3043 |     } else if (
3044 |         (parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3045 |         parent.isOr() ||
3046 |         parent.isAnd() ||
3047 |         (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3048 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3049 |     }
3050 |     return null;
3051 |   }

===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1489-1550
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1489 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1490 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1491 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1492 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1493 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1494 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1495 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1496 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1497 |         compiler.report(
1498 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1499 |       } else {
1500 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1501 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1502 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1503 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1504 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1505 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1506 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1507 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1508 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1509 |               badImplementedType = true;
1510 |             }
1511 |           } else {
1512 |             badImplementedType = true;
1513 |           }
1514 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1515 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1516 |           }
1517 |         }
1518 |         // check properties
1519 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1520 |       }
1521 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1522 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1523 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1524 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1525 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1526 |           compiler.report(
1527 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1528 |         }
1529 |       }
1530 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1531 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1532 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1533 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1534 |       }
1535 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1536 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1537 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1538 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1539 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1540 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1541 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1542 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1543 |           currentProperties.clear();
1544 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1545 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1546 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1547 |         }
1548 |       }
1549 |     }
1550 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */

67 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {

69 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));

/**

498 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,

===== M0248 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 463-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
463 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
464 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
465 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
466 |     switch (left.getType()) {
467 |       case Token.NAME:
468 |         String varName = left.getString();
469 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
470 | 
471 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
472 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
473 |         // This has two purposes:
474 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
475 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
476 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
477 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
478 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
479 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
480 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
481 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
482 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
483 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
484 |         }
485 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
486 |             resultType : null);
487 | 
488 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
489 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
490 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
491 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
492 |         }
493 |         break;
494 |       case Token.GETPROP:
495 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
496 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
497 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
498 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
499 |               resultType);
500 |         }
501 | 
502 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
503 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
504 |         break;
505 |     }
506 |   }

/**
   * When "bind" is called on a function, we infer the type of the returned
   * "bound" function by looking at the number of parameters in the call site.
   */
Method code:
889 |   private void updateBind(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
890 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Use the coding convention, so that we get goog.bind
891 |     // for free.
892 |     Node calledFn = n.getFirstChild();
893 |     boolean looksLikeBind = calledFn.isGetProp()
894 |         && calledFn.getLastChild().getString().equals("bind");
895 |     if (!looksLikeBind) {
896 |       return;
897 |     }
898 | 
899 |     Node callTarget = calledFn.getFirstChild();

901 |         .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().toMaybeFunctionType();
902 |     if (callTargetFn == null) {
903 |       return;
904 |     }
905 | 
906 |     n.setJSType(callTargetFn.getBindReturnType(n.getChildCount() - 1));

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

===== M0273 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 344-373
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
344 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
345 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
346 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
347 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
348 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
349 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
350 |         // the line numbers of both types.
351 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType, null);
352 |       } else {
353 |         // Do not type-check interface methods, because we expect that
354 |         // they will have dummy implementations that do not match the type
355 |         // annotations.
356 |         JSType ownerType = getJSType(owner);
357 |         if (ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
358 |           FunctionType ownerFn = ownerType.toObjectType().getOwnerFunction();
359 |           if (ownerFn.isInterface() &&
360 |               rightType.isFunctionType() && leftType.isFunctionType()) {
361 |             return true;
362 |           }
363 |         }
364 | 
365 |         mismatch(t, n,
366 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
367 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
368 |             rightType, leftType);
369 |       }
370 |       return false;
371 |     }
372 |     return true;
373 |   }

===== M0295 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2328-2344
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2328 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2329 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2330 |     int c;
2331 |     while (true) {
2332 |       c = stream.getChar();
2333 |       if (c == ' ') {
2334 |         continue;
2335 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2336 |         matchedLc = true;
2337 |         break;
2338 |       } else {
2339 |         break;
2340 |       }
2341 |     }
2342 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2343 |     return matchedLc;
2344 |   }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
374 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
375 |     if (prototype == null) {
376 |       return false;
377 |     }
378 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
379 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
384 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
385 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
386 | 
387 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
388 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
389 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
390 | 
391 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
392 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
393 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
394 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
398 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
399 |       if (superClass != null) {
400 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (isInterface()) {
404 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
405 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
406 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
407 |           }
408 |         }
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
413 |       clearCachedValues();
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     return true;
417 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0313 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 819-843
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
819 |   @Override
820 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
821 |     FunctionType that =
822 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
823 |     if (that == null) {
824 |       return false;
825 |     }
826 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
827 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
828 |         return this == that;
829 |       }
830 |       return false;
831 |     }
832 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
833 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
834 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
835 |       }
836 |       return false;
837 |     }
838 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
839 |       return false;
840 |     }
841 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
842 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
843 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1265-1291
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1265 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1266 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1267 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1268 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1269 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.isAssign()) {
1270 |       return;
1271 |     }
1272 | 
1273 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1274 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1275 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1276 |     // and what isn't.
1277 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1278 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1279 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1280 | 
1281 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1282 |     // non-object.
1283 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1284 |             "No properties on this expression", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1285 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1286 |       return;
1287 |     }
1288 | 
1289 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1290 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1291 |   }

===== M0351 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.registerMismatch(JSType,JSType,JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 653-677
Documentation:

Method code:
653 |   private void registerMismatch(JSType found, JSType required, JSError error) {
654 |     // Don't register a mismatch for differences in null or undefined or if the
655 |     // code didn't downcast.
656 |     found = found.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
657 |     required = required.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
658 |     if (found.canAssignTo(required) || required.canAssignTo(found)) {
659 |       return;
660 |     }
661 | 
662 |     mismatches.add(new TypeMismatch(found, required, error));
663 |     if (found.isFunctionType() &&
664 |         required.isFunctionType()) {
665 |       FunctionType fnTypeA = found.toMaybeFunctionType();
666 |       FunctionType fnTypeB = required.toMaybeFunctionType();
667 |       Iterator<Node> paramItA = fnTypeA.getParameters().iterator();
668 |       Iterator<Node> paramItB = fnTypeB.getParameters().iterator();
669 |       while (paramItA.hasNext() && paramItB.hasNext()) {
670 |         registerIfMismatch(paramItA.next().getJSType(),
671 |             paramItB.next().getJSType(), error);
672 |       }
673 | 
674 |       registerIfMismatch(
675 |         fnTypeA.getReturnType(), fnTypeB.getReturnType(), error);
676 |     }
677 |   }

   * @param key the assign node
   */
Method code:
966 |   private void visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal t, Node key, Node objlit) {
967 |     // Do not validate object lit value types in externs. We don't really care,
968 |     // and it makes it easier to generate externs.
969 |     if (objlit.isFromExterns()) {
970 |       ensureTyped(t, key);
971 |       return;
972 |     }
973 | 
974 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Validate get and set function declarations are valid
975 |     // as is the functions can have "extraneous" bits.
976 | 
977 |     // For getter and setter property definitions the
978 |     // rvalue type != the property type.
979 |     Node rvalue = key.getFirstChild();
980 |     JSType rightType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
981 |         key, getJSType(rvalue));
982 |     if (rightType == null) {
983 |       rightType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
984 |     }
985 | 
986 |     Node owner = objlit;
987 | 
988 |     // Validate value is assignable to the key type.
989 | 
990 |     JSType keyType = getJSType(key);
991 | 
992 |     JSType allowedValueType = keyType;
993 |     if (allowedValueType.isEnumElementType()) {
994 |       allowedValueType =
995 |           allowedValueType.toMaybeEnumElementType().getPrimitiveType();
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     boolean valid = validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(t, key,
999 |         rightType, allowedValueType,
1000 |         owner, NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key));
1001 |     if (valid) {
1002 |       ensureTyped(t, key, rightType);
1003 |     } else {
1004 |       ensureTyped(t, key);
1005 |     }
1006 | 
1007 |     // Validate that the key type is assignable to the object property type.
1008 |     // This is necessary as the objlit may have been cast to a non-literal
1009 |     // object type.
1010 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider introducing a CAST node to the AST (or
1011 |     // perhaps a parentheses node).
1012 | 
1013 |     JSType objlitType = getJSType(objlit);
1014 |     ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1015 |         objlitType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1016 |     if (type != null) {
1017 |       String property = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key);
1018 |       if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
1019 |           !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
1020 |           !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
1021 |         validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
1022 |             t, key, keyType,
1023 |             type.getPropertyType(property), owner, property);
1024 |       }
1025 |       return;
1026 |     }
1027 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)

/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1452 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1453 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1454 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1455 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1456 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1457 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1458 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1459 | 
1460 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1461 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1462 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1463 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1464 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1465 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1466 |       //
1467 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1468 |       // the function has jsdoc or has not been declared before.
1469 |       //
1470 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1471 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1472 | 
1473 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1474 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1475 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1476 |         // Determining type for #5
1477 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1478 |       }
1479 | 
1480 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1481 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1482 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1483 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1484 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1485 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1486 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1487 |         if (qVar != null) {
1488 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1489 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1490 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1491 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1492 |           // the @extends tag.
1493 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1494 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1495 |               rhsValue != null &&
1496 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1497 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1498 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1499 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1500 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1501 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1502 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1503 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1504 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1505 |             return;
1506 |           }
1507 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1508 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1509 |           }
1510 |         }
1511 |       }
1512 | 
1513 |       if (valueType == null) {
1514 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1515 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1516 |               n,
1517 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1518 |               ownerName));
1519 |         }
1520 | 
1521 |         return;
1522 |       }
1523 | 
1524 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1525 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1526 |       if (!inferred) {
1527 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1528 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1529 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1530 |           // declared yet.
1531 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1532 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1533 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1534 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1535 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1536 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1537 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1538 |           }
1539 |         }
1540 | 
1541 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1542 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1543 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1544 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1545 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1546 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1547 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1548 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1549 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1550 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1551 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1552 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1553 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1554 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1555 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1556 |           }
1557 |         }
1558 |       }
1559 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
