Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1659 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1660 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1661 | 
1662 |     // This is a misplaced return, but the real JS will fail to compile,
1663 |     // so let it go.
1664 |     if (function == null) {
1665 |       return;
1666 |     }
1667 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(function);
1668 | 
1669 |     if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
1670 |       FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1671 | 
1672 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1673 | 
1674 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1675 |       // (it's a void function)
1676 |       if (returnType == null) {
1677 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1678 |       }
1679 | 
1680 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1681 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1682 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1683 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1684 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1685 |         valueNode = n;
1686 |       } else {
1687 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1688 |       }
1689 | 
1690 |       // verifying
1691 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1692 |           "inconsistent return type");
1693 |     }
1694 |   }

===== M0382 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 135-247
Documentation:

Method code:
135 |   @Override
136 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
137 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
138 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
139 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
140 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
141 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
142 |     // worthwhile.
143 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
144 |     Node condition = null;
145 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
146 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
147 | 
148 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
149 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
150 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
151 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
152 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
153 | 
154 |       switch (branch) {
155 |         case ON_TRUE:
156 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
157 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
158 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
159 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
160 | 
161 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
162 | 
163 |             if (item.isVar()) {
164 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
165 |             }
166 |             if (item.isName()) {
167 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
168 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
169 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
170 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
171 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
172 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
173 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
174 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
175 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
176 |                 }
177 |               }
178 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
179 |             }
180 |             newScope = informed;
181 |             break;
182 |           }
183 | 
184 |           // FALL THROUGH
185 | 
186 |         case ON_FALSE:
187 |           if (condition == null) {
188 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
189 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
190 |               condition = source;
191 | 
192 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
193 |               // of the loop.
194 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
195 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
196 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
197 |               }
198 |             }
199 |           }
200 | 
201 |           if (condition != null) {
202 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
203 |                 condition.isOr()) {
204 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
205 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
206 |               // scope on false.
207 |               //
208 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
209 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
210 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
211 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
212 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
213 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
214 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
215 | 
216 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
217 |               // of the loop.
218 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
219 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
220 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
221 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
222 |               }
223 |               newScope =
224 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
225 |                       condition,
226 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
227 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
228 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
229 |             } else {
230 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
231 |               // of the loop.
232 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
233 |                 conditionFlowScope =
234 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
235 |               }
236 |               newScope =
237 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
238 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
239 |             }
240 |           }
241 |           break;
242 |       }
243 | 
244 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
245 |     }
246 |     return result;
247 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 913-938
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
913 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
914 |     int i = 0;
915 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
916 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
917 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
918 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
919 |         return;
920 |       }
921 | 
922 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
923 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
924 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
925 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
926 | 
927 |       if (iParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
928 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = iParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
929 | 
930 |         if (iArgument.isFunction() &&
931 |             iArgumentType.isFunctionType() &&
932 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
933 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       i++;
937 |     }
938 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1078-1091
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
1078 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1079 |     if (resolved) {
1080 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
1081 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
1082 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
1083 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1084 |       }
1085 |       return resolveResult;
1086 |     }
1087 |     resolved = true;
1088 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
1089 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
1090 |     return resolveResult;
1091 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1049-1150
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1049 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1050 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1051 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1052 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1053 |     // already.
1054 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1055 |       return;
1056 |     }
1057 | 
1058 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1059 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1060 |         superClass.getInstanceType().hasProperty(propertyName);
1061 |     // For interface
1062 |     boolean superInterfacesHasProperty = false;
1063 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1064 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1065 |         superInterfacesHasProperty =
1066 |           superInterfacesHasProperty || interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1067 |       }
1068 |     }
1069 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1070 | 
1071 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1072 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1073 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1074 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1075 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1076 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1077 |           continue;
1078 |         }
1079 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1080 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1081 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1082 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1083 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1084 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1085 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1086 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1087 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1088 |           // property
1089 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1090 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1091 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1092 |         }
1093 |       }
1094 |     }
1095 | 
1096 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty
1097 |         && !superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1098 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1099 |       return;
1100 |     }
1101 | 
1102 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1103 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1104 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1105 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1106 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1107 |       // property
1108 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1109 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1110 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1111 |     }
1112 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1113 |       // there's no @override to check
1114 |       return;
1115 |     }
1116 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1117 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1118 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1119 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1120 |           superClass.getInstanceType().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1121 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1122 |         compiler.report(
1123 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1124 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1125 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1126 |       }
1127 |     } else if (superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1128 |       // there is an super interface property
1129 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1130 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1131 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1132 |             interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1133 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1134 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1135 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1136 |             compiler.report(
1137 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1138 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1139 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1140 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1141 |           }
1142 |         }
1143 |       }
1144 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1145 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1146 |       compiler.report(
1147 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1148 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1149 |     }
1150 |   }

===== M0424 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0438 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0453 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitBinaryOperator(int,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1706-1760
Documentation:
/**
   * This function unifies the type checking involved in the core binary
   * operators and the corresponding assignment operators.  The representation
   * used internally is such that common code can handle both kinds of
   * operators easily.
   *
   * @param op The operator.
   * @param t The traversal object, needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node being checked.
   */
Method code:
1706 |   private void visitBinaryOperator(int op, NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1707 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
1708 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(left);
1709 |     Node right = n.getLastChild();
1710 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(right);
1711 |     switch (op) {
1712 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1713 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1714 |       case Token.LSH:
1715 |       case Token.RSH:
1716 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1717 |       case Token.URSH:
1718 |         if (!leftType.matchesInt32Context()) {
1719 |           report(t, left, BIT_OPERATION,
1720 |                    NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()), leftType.toString());
1721 |         }
1722 |         if (!rightType.matchesUint32Context()) {
1723 |           report(t, right, BIT_OPERATION,
1724 |                    NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()), rightType.toString());
1725 |         }
1726 |         break;
1727 | 
1728 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1729 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1730 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1731 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1732 |       case Token.DIV:
1733 |       case Token.MOD:
1734 |       case Token.MUL:
1735 |       case Token.SUB:
1736 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, leftType, "left operand");
1737 |         validator.expectNumber(t, right, rightType, "right operand");
1738 |         break;
1739 | 
1740 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1741 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1742 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1743 |       case Token.BITAND:
1744 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1745 |       case Token.BITOR:
1746 |         validator.expectBitwiseable(t, left, leftType,
1747 |             "bad left operand to bitwise operator");
1748 |         validator.expectBitwiseable(t, right, rightType,
1749 |             "bad right operand to bitwise operator");
1750 |         break;
1751 | 
1752 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1753 |       case Token.ADD:
1754 |         break;
1755 | 
1756 |       default:
1757 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(op));
1758 |     }
1759 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1760 |   }

===== M0454 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 460-475
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
460 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
461 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
462 |       Set<String> directives = null;
463 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
464 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
465 |         if (directives == null) {
466 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
467 |         } else {
468 |           directives.add(directive);
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       if (directives != null) {
473 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
474 |       }
475 |     }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1226-1255
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1226 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1227 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1228 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1229 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1230 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1231 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1232 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1233 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1234 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1235 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1236 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1237 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1238 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1239 |       return false;
1240 |     }
1241 | 
1242 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1243 |     if (type == null) {
1244 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1245 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1246 |       if (var != null) {
1247 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1248 |         if (varType != null) {
1249 |           type = varType;
1250 |         }
1251 |       }
1252 |     }
1253 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1254 |     return true;
1255 |   }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

===== M0461 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2072-2098
Documentation:
/**
   * @param key A OBJECTLIT key node.
   * @return The type expected when using the key.
   */
Method code:
2072 |   static JSType getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node key, JSType valueType) {
2073 |     if (valueType != null) {
2074 |       switch (key.getType()) {
2075 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
2076 |           // GET must always return a function type.
2077 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2078 |             FunctionType fntype = valueType.toMaybeFunctionType();
2079 |             valueType = fntype.getReturnType();
2080 |           } else {
2081 |             return null;
2082 |           }
2083 |           break;
2084 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
2085 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2086 |             // SET must always return a function type.
2087 |             FunctionType fntype = valueType.toMaybeFunctionType();
2088 |             Node param = fntype.getParametersNode().getFirstChild();
2089 |             // SET function must always have one parameter.
2090 |             valueType = param.getJSType();
2091 |           } else {
2092 |             return null;
2093 |           }
2094 |           break;
2095 |       }
2096 |     }
2097 |     return valueType;
2098 |   }

950 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
951 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
952 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
953 |       // on a superclass.
954 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);

===== M0474 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.isTry() &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1300-1327
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1300 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1301 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1302 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1303 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1304 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1305 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1306 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1307 |     if (propType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1308 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1309 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1310 |       if (objectType != null) {
1311 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1312 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1313 |         // faster in most cases).
1314 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1315 |             objectType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1316 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1317 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1318 |           } else {
1319 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1320 |           }
1321 |         }
1322 | 
1323 |       } else {
1324 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1325 |       }
1326 |     }
1327 |   }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 238-301
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
238 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
239 |     this.options = options;
240 |     if (errorManager == null) {
241 |       if (outStream == null) {
242 |         setErrorManager(
243 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
244 |       } else {
245 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
246 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
247 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
248 |         setErrorManager(printer);
249 |       }
250 |     }
251 | 
252 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
253 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
254 |       options.checkTypes = true;
255 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
256 |       options.checkTypes = false;
257 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
258 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
259 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
260 |       // parser type warnings.
261 |       options.setWarningLevel(
262 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
263 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
264 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
265 |     }
266 | 
267 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
268 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
271 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
272 |     }
273 | 
274 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
275 |       options.setWarningLevel(
276 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
277 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
281 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
282 |     guards.add(
283 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
284 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
285 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
286 | 
287 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
288 | 
289 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
290 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
291 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
292 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
293 |     // error off.
294 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
295 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
296 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
297 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
298 |     }
299 | 
300 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
301 |   }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 879-883
Documentation:
/**
   * We only do forward type inference. We do not do full backwards
   * type inference.
   *
   * In other words, if we have,
   * <code>
   * var x = f();
   * g(x);
   * </code>
   * a forward type-inference engine would try to figure out the type
   * of "x" from the return type of "f". A backwards type-inference engine
   * would try to figure out the type of "x" from the parameter type of "g".
   *
   * However, there are a few special syntactic forms where we do some
   * some half-assed backwards type-inference, because programmers
   * expect it in this day and age. To take an example from java,
   * <code>
   * List<String> x = Lists.newArrayList();
   * </code>
   * The Java compiler will be able to infer the generic type of the List
   * returned by newArrayList().
   *
   * In much the same way, we do some special-case backwards inference for
   * JS. Those cases are enumerated here.
   */
Method code:
879 |   private void backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
880 |     updateTypeOfParameters(n, fnType);
881 |     updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(n, fnType);
882 |     updateBind(n, fnType);
883 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 595-609
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
603 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
604 |       scopes.push(scope);
605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFieldType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2120-2148
Documentation:
/**
   * FieldType := FieldName | FieldName ':' TypeExpression
   */
Method code:
2120 |   private Node parseFieldType(JsDocToken token) {
2121 |     Node fieldName = parseFieldName(token);
2122 | 
2123 |     if (fieldName == null) {
2124 |       return null;
2125 |     }
2126 | 
2127 |     skipEOLs();
2128 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
2129 |       return fieldName;
2130 |     }
2131 | 
2132 |     // Move to the colon.
2133 |     next();
2134 | 
2135 |     // Move to the token after the colon and parse
2136 |     // the type expression.
2137 |     skipEOLs();
2138 |     Node typeExpression = parseTypeExpression(next());
2139 | 
2140 |     if (typeExpression == null) {
2141 |       return null;
2142 |     }
2143 | 
2144 |     Node fieldType = newNode(Token.COLON);
2145 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(fieldName);
2146 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(typeExpression);
2147 |     return fieldType;
2148 |   }

===== M0560 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0565 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0582 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-221
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |         return n.getString();
182 | 
183 |       case Token.NAME:
184 |         String name = n.getString();
185 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
186 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
187 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
188 |           return name;
189 |         }
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NUMBER:
193 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
194 | 
195 |       case Token.FALSE:
196 |         return "false";
197 | 
198 |       case Token.TRUE:
199 |         return "true";
200 | 
201 |       case Token.NULL:
202 |         return "null";
203 | 
204 |       case Token.VOID:
205 |         return "undefined";
206 | 
207 |       case Token.NOT:
208 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
209 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
210 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
211 |         }
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
215 |         return arrayToString(n);
216 | 
217 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
218 |         return "[object Object]";
219 |     }
220 |     return null;
221 |   }

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 514-574
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   * @return The variable we end up with. Most of the time, this will just
   *     be {@code var}, but in some rare cases we will need to declare
   *     a new var with new source info.
   */
Method code:
514 |   Var expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
515 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
516 |     Var newVar = var;
517 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
518 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
519 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
520 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
521 |       if (info == null) {
522 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
523 |       }
524 |       allowDupe =
525 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
526 |     }
527 | 
528 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
529 | 
530 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
531 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
532 |     // compilation process.
533 |     if (varType != null &&
534 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
535 |         newType != null &&
536 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
537 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
538 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
539 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
540 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
541 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
542 |       if (var.input == null) {
543 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
544 |         s.undeclare(var);
545 |         newVar = s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
546 | 
547 |         n.setJSType(varType);
548 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
549 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
550 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
551 |           }
552 |         } else {
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
554 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
555 |         }
556 |       } else {
557 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
558 |         // match the original type.
559 |         //
560 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
561 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
562 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
563 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
564 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
565 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
566 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
567 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
568 |               varType.toString()));
569 |         }
570 |       }
571 |     }
572 | 
573 |     return newVar;
574 |   }

   * Gets the number of properties of this object.

Method code:
151 |   @Override
152 |   public int getPropertiesCount() {
153 |     ObjectType implicitPrototype = getImplicitPrototype();
154 |     if (implicitPrototype == null) {

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0595 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 690-787
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
690 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
691 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
692 |     /*
693 |      * This is the case where:
694 |      *
695 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
696 |      * the script.
697 |      *
698 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
699 |      * the caller of the function.
700 |      *
701 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
702 |      * back to the caller of the function.
703 |      *
704 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
705 |      * asked to traverse.
706 |      *
707 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
708 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
709 |      */
710 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
711 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
712 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
713 |       return null;
714 |     }
715 | 
716 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
717 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
718 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
719 |       case Token.IF:
720 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
723 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
724 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
725 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
726 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
727 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
728 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
730 |           } else {
731 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
732 |           }
733 |         } else {
734 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
735 |         }
736 |         break;
737 |       case Token.FOR:
738 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
739 |           return parent;
740 |         } else {
741 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
742 |         }
743 |       case Token.WHILE:
744 |       case Token.DO:
745 |         return parent;
746 |       case Token.TRY:
747 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
748 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
749 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
750 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
751 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
752 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
753 |           }
754 |         // CATCH block.
755 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
756 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
757 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
758 |           } else {
759 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
760 |           }
761 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
762 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
763 |           if (cfa != null) {
764 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
765 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
766 |             }
767 |           }
768 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
769 |         }
770 |     }
771 | 
772 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
773 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
774 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
775 | 
776 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
777 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
778 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
782 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
783 |     } else {
784 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
785 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
786 |     }
787 |   }

/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param type The type of this if the info is missing.
   */
Method code:

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1616-1650
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1616 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1617 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1618 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1619 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1620 | 
1621 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1622 |     int ordinal = 0;
1623 |     Node parameter = null;
1624 |     Node argument = null;
1625 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1626 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1627 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1628 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1629 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1630 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1631 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1632 |       }
1633 |       argument = arguments.next();
1634 |       ordinal++;
1635 | 
1636 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1637 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1638 |     }
1639 | 
1640 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1641 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1642 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1643 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1644 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1645 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1646 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1647 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1648 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1649 |     }
1650 |   }

   * the actual type of T.

Method code:
945 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
946 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
947 | 
948 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
949 |       return;
950 |     }
951 | 
952 |     int i = 0;
953 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
954 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
955 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
956 |       JSType iParameterType =
957 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
958 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
959 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
960 |         ObjectType iArgumentType = null;
961 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
962 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
963 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument)
964 |               .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
965 |               .collapseUnion()
966 |               .toObjectType();
967 |           if (iArgumentType == null) {
968 |             compiler.report(
969 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
970 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE,
971 |                     getJSType(iArgument).toString()));
972 |             return;
973 |           }
974 |         }
975 | 
976 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
977 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
978 |         int j = 0;
979 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
980 |           JSType jParameterType =
981 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
982 |           if (jParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
983 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = jParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
984 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
985 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
986 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
987 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
988 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
989 |                 return;
990 |               }
991 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
992 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
993 |               if (jArgument.isFunction() &&
994 |                   jArgumentType.isFunctionType()) {
995 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
996 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
997 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
998 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
999 |                   // using the actual type of T.
1000 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = jArgumentType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1001 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
1002 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
1003 |                     // function.
1004 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
1005 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
1006 |                             jArgumentFnType, iArgumentType));
1007 |                   }
1008 |                 } else {
1009 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
1010 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
1011 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
1012 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
1013 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
1014 |                   }
1015 |                 }
1016 |               }
1017 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
1018 |               // types of the arguments match.
1019 |             }
1020 |           }
1021 |           j++;
1022 |         }
1023 | 
1024 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
1025 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
1026 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
1027 |           return;
1028 |         }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)

Original range: 1747-1782
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1747 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1748 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1749 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1750 |     }
1751 | 
1752 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1753 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1754 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1755 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1756 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1757 |       skipEOLs();
1758 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1759 |         next();
1760 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1761 |       }
1762 |     }
1763 | 
1764 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1765 | 
1766 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1767 |       next();
1768 |       skipEOLs();
1769 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1770 |       if (memberType != null) {
1771 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1772 | 
1773 |         skipEOLs();
1774 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1775 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1776 |         }
1777 | 
1778 |         next();
1779 |       }
1780 |     }
1781 |     return typeNameNode;
1782 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 833-955
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
833 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
834 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
835 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
836 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
837 | 
838 |     // Check property sets to 'object.property' when 'object' is known.
839 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
840 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
841 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
842 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
843 | 
844 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
845 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
846 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
847 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
848 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
849 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
850 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
851 |         }
852 |       }
853 | 
854 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
855 | 
856 |       // Prototype assignments are special, because they actually affect
857 |       // the definition of a class. These are mostly validated
858 |       // during TypedScopeCreator, and we only look for the "dumb" cases here.
859 |       // object.prototype = ...;
860 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
861 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
862 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
863 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
864 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
865 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
866 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
867 |             return;
868 |           }
869 |         }
870 |       }
871 | 
872 |       // Inheritance checks for prototype properties.
873 |       //
874 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This isn't the right place to do this check. We
875 |       // really want to do this when we're looking at the constructor.
876 |       // We'd find all its properties and make sure they followed inheritance
877 |       // rules, like we currently do for @implements to make sure
878 |       // all the methods are implemented.
879 |       //
880 |       // As-is, this misses many other ways to override a property.
881 |       //
882 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
883 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
884 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
885 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
886 | 
887 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
888 |           JSType jsType = getJSType(object2);
889 |           if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
890 |             FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
891 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
892 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
893 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
894 |             }
895 |           }
896 |         }
897 |       }
898 | 
899 |       // The generic checks for 'object.property' when 'object' is known,
900 |       // and 'property' is declared on it.
901 |       // object.property = ...;
902 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
903 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
904 |       if (type != null) {
905 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
906 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
907 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
908 |           JSType expectedType = type.getPropertyType(property);
909 |           if (!expectedType.isUnknownType()) {
910 |             validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
911 |                 t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
912 |                 expectedType, object, property);
913 |             return;
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 |       }
917 |     }
918 | 
919 |     // Check qualified name sets to 'object' and 'object.property'.
920 |     // This can sometimes handle cases when the type of 'object' is not known.
921 |     // e.g.,
922 |     // var obj = createUnknownType();
923 |     // /** @type {number} */ obj.foo = true;
924 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
925 |     if (lvalue.isQualifiedName()) {
926 |       // variable with inferred type case
927 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
928 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getQualifiedName());
929 |       if (var != null) {
930 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
931 |           return;
932 |         }
933 | 
934 |         if (NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(lvalue).isThis() &&
935 |             t.getScope() != var.getScope()) {
936 |           // Don't look at "this.foo" variables from other scopes.
937 |           return;
938 |         }
939 | 
940 |         if (var.getType() != null) {
941 |           leftType = var.getType();
942 |         }
943 |       }
944 |     }
945 | 
946 |     // Fall through case for arbitrary LHS and arbitrary RHS.
947 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
948 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

===== M0630 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1141-1175
Documentation:

Method code:
1141 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1142 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1143 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1144 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1145 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1146 |     if (var != null) {
1147 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1148 |       if (varType != null) {
1149 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1150 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1151 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1152 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1153 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1154 |         } else {
1155 |           return varType;
1156 |         }
1157 |       }
1158 |     }
1159 | 
1160 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1161 |     if (objType != null) {
1162 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1163 |     }
1164 | 
1165 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1166 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1167 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1168 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1169 |       if (regType != null) {
1170 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1171 |       }
1172 |     }
1173 | 
1174 |     return propertyType;
1175 |   }
