===== M0633 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 136-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
136 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
137 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
138 |     String methodName = null;
139 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
140 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
141 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
142 |       String name = callName.getString();
143 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
144 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
145 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
146 |       }
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     if (methodName != null) {
150 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
151 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
152 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
153 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
154 |       }
155 |     }
156 |     return null;
157 |   }

/**
   * <p>Checks enum aliases.
   *
   * <p>We verify that the enum element type of the enum used
   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *
   * <p>Example:</p>
   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>
   *
   * <p>Enum aliases are irregular, so we need special code for this :(</p>
   *
   * @param value the value used for initialization of the enum
   */
Method code:
1777 |   private void checkEnumAlias(
1778 |       NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo declInfo, Node value) {
1779 |     if (declInfo == null || !declInfo.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1780 |       return;
1781 |     }
1782 | 
1783 |     JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1784 |     if (!valueType.isEnumType()) {
1785 |       return;
1786 |     }
1787 | 
1788 |     EnumType valueEnumType = valueType.toMaybeEnumType();
1789 |     JSType valueEnumPrimitiveType =
1790 |         valueEnumType.getElementsType().getPrimitiveType();
1791 |     validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, value, valueEnumPrimitiveType,
1792 |         declInfo.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry),
1793 |         "incompatible enum element types");
1794 |   }

===== M0652 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0655 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 794-808
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
794 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
795 |     switch (n.getType()) {
796 |       case Token.DO:
797 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
798 |       case Token.FOR:
799 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
800 |           return n;
801 |         }
802 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
803 |       case Token.LABEL:
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
805 |       default:
806 |         return n;
807 |     }
808 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)

Method code:
799 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
800 |       Node callNode) {
801 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
802 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
803 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
804 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
805 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
806 |       return scope;
807 |     }
808 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
809 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
810 |       return scope;
811 |     }
812 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
813 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
814 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
815 |     if (assertedType == null) {
816 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
817 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
818 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
819 |         if (type != narrowed) {
820 |           scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
821 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
822 |         }
823 |       } else if (assertedNode.isAnd() ||
824 |                  assertedNode.isOr()) {
825 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
826 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
827 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
828 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
829 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
830 |       }
831 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
832 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
833 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
834 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
835 |       if (type != narrowed) {
836 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
837 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 |     return scope;
841 |   }

616 |     @Override
617 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
618 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
619 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
620 |       if (name == null) {
621 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
622 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
623 |           errorReporter.error(
624 |             "unnamed function statement",
625 |             sourceName,
626 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
627 | 
628 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
629 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
630 |         }
631 |         name = new Name();
632 |         name.setIdentifier("");
633 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
634 |       }
635 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
636 |       Node newName = transform(name);
637 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
638 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
639 |         // declaration.
640 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
641 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
642 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
643 |         // same line as the function keyword.
644 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
645 |             functionNode.getLp();
646 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
647 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
648 |       }
649 | 
650 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
651 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
652 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
653 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
654 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
655 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
656 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
657 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
658 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
659 |       if (fnName != null) {
660 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
661 |       } else {
662 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
663 |       }
664 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
665 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
666 | 
667 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
668 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
669 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
670 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
671 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
672 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
673 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
674 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
675 |         } else {
676 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
677 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
678 |           // (an error has already been reported).
679 |           Preconditions.checkState(
680 |               config.isIdeMode
681 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
682 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
683 |         }
684 |       }
685 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
686 | 
687 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
688 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
689 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
690 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
691 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
692 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
693 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
694 |       }
695 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
696 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
697 |      return node;
698 |     }

===== M0666 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.isQualifiedNameInferred(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1588-1620
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a qualified name is inferred.
     * NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
     * is really really obnoxious.
     *
     * The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
     *
     * (function() {
     *   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * })();
     *
     * function f() {
     *   goog.bar();
     *   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * }
     *
     * In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
     * to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
     * contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
     * of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
     *
     * The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
     * jsdoc type information, or @const with a known type,
     * or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
     */
Method code:
1588 |     private boolean isQualifiedNameInferred(
1589 |         String qName, Node n, JSDocInfo info,
1590 |         Node rhsValue, JSType valueType) {
1591 |       if (valueType == null) {
1592 |         return true;
1593 |       }
1594 | 
1595 |       boolean inferred = true;
1596 |       if (info != null) {
1597 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1598 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1599 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1600 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1601 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1602 |       }
1603 | 
1604 |       if (inferred && rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isFunction()) {
1605 |         if (info != null) {
1606 |           inferred = false;
1607 |         } else if (!scope.isDeclared(qName, false) &&
1608 |             n.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
1609 | 
1610 |           // Check if this is assigned in an inner scope.
1611 |           AstFunctionContents contents =
1612 |               getFunctionAnalysisResults(scope.getRootNode());
1613 |           if (contents == null ||
1614 |               !contents.getEscapedQualifiedNames().contains(qName)) {
1615 |             inferred = false;
1616 |           }
1617 |         }
1618 |       }
1619 |       return inferred;
1620 |     }

794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)

/**
   * Creates a RecordType from the nodes representing said record type.
   * @param n The node with type info.
   * @param sourceName The source file name.
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name lookups.
   */
Method code:
1614 |   private JSType createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node n, String sourceName,
1615 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1616 | 
1617 |     RecordTypeBuilder builder = new RecordTypeBuilder(this);
1618 | 
1619 |     // For each of the fields in the record type.
1620 |     for (Node fieldTypeNode = n.getFirstChild();
1621 |          fieldTypeNode != null;
1622 |          fieldTypeNode = fieldTypeNode.getNext()) {
1623 | 
1624 |       // Get the property's name.
1625 |       Node fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode;
1626 |       boolean hasType = false;
1627 | 
1628 |       if (fieldTypeNode.getType() == Token.COLON) {
1629 |         fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode.getFirstChild();
1630 |         hasType = true;
1631 |       }
1632 | 
1633 |       String fieldName = fieldNameNode.getString();
1634 | 
1635 |       // TODO(user): Move this into the lexer/parser.
1636 |       // Remove the string literal characters around a field name,
1637 |       // if any.
1638 |       if (fieldName.startsWith("'") || fieldName.startsWith("\"")) {
1639 |         fieldName = fieldName.substring(1, fieldName.length() - 1);
1640 |       }
1641 | 
1642 |       // Get the property's type.
1643 |       JSType fieldType = null;
1644 | 
1645 |       if (hasType) {
1646 |         // We have a declared type.
1647 |         fieldType = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1648 |             fieldTypeNode.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1649 |       } else {
1650 |         // Otherwise, the type is UNKNOWN.
1651 |         fieldType = getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1652 |       }
1653 | 
1654 |       // Add the property to the record.
1655 |       if (builder.addProperty(fieldName, fieldType, fieldNameNode) == null) {
1656 |         // Duplicate field name, warning and skip
1657 |         reporter.warning(
1658 |             "Duplicate record field " + fieldName,
1659 |             sourceName,
1660 |             n.getLineno(), fieldNameNode.getCharno());
1661 |       }
1662 |     }
1663 | 
1664 |     return builder.build();
1665 |   }

===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 460-804
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
460 |   @Override
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (!parent.isFunction()) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.NULL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.NUMBER:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.STRING:
505 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
506 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
507 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
508 |         } else {
509 |           // Object literal keys are not typeable
510 |           typeable = false;
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
515 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
516 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
517 |         break;
518 | 
519 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
520 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.REGEXP:
524 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.GETPROP:
528 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
529 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
530 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.GETELEM:
534 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
535 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
536 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
537 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
538 |         typeable = false;
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.VAR:
542 |         visitVar(t, n);
543 |         typeable = false;
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NEW:
547 |         visitNew(t, n);
548 |         typeable = true;
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.CALL:
552 |         visitCall(t, n);
553 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.RETURN:
557 |         visitReturn(t, n);
558 |         typeable = false;
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.DEC:
562 |       case Token.INC:
563 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
564 |         validator.expectNumber(
565 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
566 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NOT:
570 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.VOID:
574 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
578 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.BITNOT:
582 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
583 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
584 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
585 |               childType.toString());
586 |         }
587 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.POS:
591 |       case Token.NEG:
592 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
593 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
594 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
595 |         break;
596 | 
597 |       case Token.EQ:
598 |       case Token.NE: {
599 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
600 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
601 | 
602 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
603 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
604 |         TernaryValue result =
605 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
606 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
607 |           if (n.isNE()) {
608 |             result = result.not();
609 |           }
610 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
611 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
612 |         }
613 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
614 |         break;
615 |       }
616 | 
617 |       case Token.SHEQ:
618 |       case Token.SHNE: {
619 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
620 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
621 | 
622 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
623 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
624 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
625 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
626 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
627 |               rightType.toString());
628 |         }
629 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
630 |         break;
631 |       }
632 | 
633 |       case Token.LT:
634 |       case Token.LE:
635 |       case Token.GT:
636 |       case Token.GE:
637 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
638 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
639 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
640 |           validator.expectNumber(
641 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
642 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
643 |           validator.expectNumber(
644 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
645 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
646 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
647 |           // OK.
648 |         } else {
649 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
650 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
651 |           // should match a string context.
652 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
653 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
654 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
655 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
656 |           message = "right side of comparison";
657 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
658 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
659 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
660 |         }
661 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
662 |         break;
663 | 
664 |       case Token.IN:
665 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
666 |         right = n.getLastChild();
667 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
668 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
669 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
670 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
671 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
672 |         break;
673 | 
674 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
675 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
676 |         right = n.getLastChild();
677 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
678 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
679 | 
680 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
681 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
682 |         validator.expectActualObject(
683 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
684 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
685 |         break;
686 | 
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
688 |         visitAssign(t, n);
689 |         typeable = false;
690 |         break;
691 | 
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
703 |       case Token.LSH:
704 |       case Token.RSH:
705 |       case Token.URSH:
706 |       case Token.DIV:
707 |       case Token.MOD:
708 |       case Token.BITOR:
709 |       case Token.BITXOR:
710 |       case Token.BITAND:
711 |       case Token.SUB:
712 |       case Token.ADD:
713 |       case Token.MUL:
714 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
715 |         break;
716 | 
717 |       case Token.DELPROP:
718 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
723 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
724 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
725 |         typeable = false;
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.WITH: {
729 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
730 |         childType = getJSType(child);
731 |         validator.expectObject(
732 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
733 |         typeable = false;
734 |         break;
735 |       }
736 | 
737 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
738 |         visitFunction(t, n);
739 |         break;
740 | 
741 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
742 |       case Token.LABEL:
743 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
744 |       case Token.SWITCH:
745 |       case Token.BREAK:
746 |       case Token.CATCH:
747 |       case Token.TRY:
748 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
749 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
750 |       case Token.BLOCK:
751 |       case Token.EMPTY:
752 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
753 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
754 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
755 |       case Token.THROW:
756 |         typeable = false;
757 |         break;
758 | 
759 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
760 |       case Token.DO:
761 |       case Token.FOR:
762 |       case Token.IF:
763 |       case Token.WHILE:
764 |         typeable = false;
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
768 |       case Token.AND:
769 |       case Token.HOOK:
770 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
771 |       case Token.OR:
772 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
773 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
774 |         } else {
775 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
776 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
777 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
778 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
779 |           } else {
780 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
781 |           }
782 |         }
783 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
784 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
785 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
786 |           }
787 |         }
788 |         break;
789 | 
790 |       default:
791 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
792 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
793 |         break;
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
797 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
798 | 
799 |     if (typeable) {
800 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
801 |     }
802 | 
803 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
804 |   }

===== M0702 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 181-949
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
181 |   boolean parse() {
182 |     int lineno;
183 |     int charno;
184 | 
185 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
186 |     JSTypeExpression type;
187 | 
188 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
189 |     skipEOLs();
190 | 
191 |     JsDocToken token = next();
192 | 
193 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
194 | 
195 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
196 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
197 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
198 |       token = blockInfo.token;
199 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
206 |         // what it was.
207 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
212 |     retry: for (;;) {
213 |       switch (token) {
214 |         case ANNOTATION:
215 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
216 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
217 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
218 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
219 | 
220 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
221 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
222 |             if (annotation == null) {
223 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
225 |             } else {
226 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
227 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
228 | 
229 |               switch (annotation) {
230 |                 case AUTHOR:
231 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
232 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
233 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
234 | 
235 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
236 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
237 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                     } else {
239 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
240 |                     }
241 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
242 |                   } else {
243 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
244 |                   }
245 |                   continue retry;
246 | 
247 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
250 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;
254 | 
255 |                 case CONSTANT:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
257 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
258 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
261 |                   continue retry;
262 | 
263 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
265 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
266 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
267 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
268 |                     } else {
269 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
270 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
271 |                     }
272 |                   }
273 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
274 |                   continue retry;
275 | 
276 |                 case DEPRECATED:
277 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
278 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }
281 | 
282 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
283 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
284 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
285 | 
286 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
287 | 
288 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
289 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
290 |                   }
291 | 
292 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
293 |                   continue retry;
294 | 
295 |                 case INTERFACE:
296 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
297 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
298 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
299 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
300 |                     } else {
301 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
302 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
303 |                     }
304 |                   }
305 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
306 |                   continue retry;
307 | 
308 |                 case DESC:
309 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
310 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
311 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
312 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
313 |                     continue retry;
314 |                   } else {
315 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
316 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
317 | 
318 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
319 | 
320 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
321 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
322 |                     continue retry;
323 |                   }
324 | 
325 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
326 |                   String fileOverview = "";
327 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
328 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
329 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
330 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
331 | 
332 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
333 | 
334 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
335 |                   } else {
336 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
337 |                   }
338 | 
339 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
340 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
341 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;
345 | 
346 |                 case LICENSE:
347 |                 case PRESERVE:
348 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
350 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
353 | 
354 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
355 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
356 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
357 |                     }
358 |                   }
359 | 
360 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
361 |                   continue retry;
362 | 
363 |                 case ENUM:
364 |                   token = next();
365 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
366 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
367 | 
368 |                   type = null;
369 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
370 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
371 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
372 |                   }
373 | 
374 |                   if (type == null) {

459 |                       }

463 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
464 |                     }

467 |                   }

469 |                   continue retry;

474 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
475 |                   }

479 |                 case LENDS:
480 |                   skipEOLs();

492 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                     }

496 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
497 |                   }

514 |                   }

536 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
537 |                   }

567 |                       // recovering parsing

574 |                   token = current();

588 |                     }

597 |                   skipEOLs();

599 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
600 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
601 |                   type = null;
602 | 
603 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
604 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
605 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));

607 |                     if (type == null) {
608 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
609 |                       // recovering parsing
610 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
611 |                       continue retry;
612 |                     }
613 |                     skipEOLs();
614 |                     token = next();
615 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
616 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
617 |                   }

620 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
621 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
622 |                     token = next();
623 |                   }

626 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
627 |                         lineno, charno);

632 |                       token = next();

634 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
635 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type

637 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
638 |                         token = next();
639 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {

645 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");

648 |                         // already.

654 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method

657 |                       name = null;
658 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
659 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
660 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,

664 |                             lineno, charno);

671 |                     continue retry;

675 |

678 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
679 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
680 | 
681 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
682 | 
683 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
684 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
685 |                           paramDescription);
686 |                     }

689 |                   } else {

691 |                   }
692 |                   continue retry;
693 | 
694 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
695 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
696 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
697 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
698 |                   }

701 |

847 |                     // recovering parsing

852 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
853 |                               lineno, charno);
854 |                         }

857 |                       case RETURN:

859 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(

861 |                           break;
862 |                         }

865 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {

867 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
868 | 
869 |                           String returnDescription =
870 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;

872 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
873 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
874 |                                 returnDescription);
875 |                           }
876 | 
877 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
878 |                         } else {
879 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
880 |                         }
881 |                         continue retry;
882 | 
883 |                       case THIS:
884 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
885 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
886 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
887 |                         }
888 |                         break;
889 | 
890 |                       case TYPE:
891 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
892 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
893 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
894 |                         }
895 |                         break;
896 | 
897 |                       case TYPEDEF:
898 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
899 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
900 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
901 |                         }
902 |                         break;
903 |                     }
904 |                   }
905 | 
906 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
907 |                   continue retry;
908 |               }
909 |             }
910 |           }
911 |           break;
912 | 
913 |         case EOC:
914 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
915 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
916 |           }
917 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
918 |           return true;
919 | 
920 |         case EOF:
921 |           // discard any accumulated information
922 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
923 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
924 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
925 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
926 |           return false;
927 | 
928 |         case EOL:
929 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
930 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
931 |           }
932 |           token = next();
933 |           continue retry;
934 | 
935 |         default:
936 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
937 |             token = next();
938 |             continue retry;
939 |           } else {
940 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
941 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
942 |             continue retry;
943 |           }
944 |       }
945 | 
946 |       // next token
947 |       token = next();
948 |     }
949 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1452-1606
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1452 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1453 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1455 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1456 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1457 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1458 | 
1459 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1460 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1461 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1462 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1463 | 
1464 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1465 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1466 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1467 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1468 |         }
1469 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1470 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1471 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1472 | 
1473 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1474 |         return createOptionalType(
1475 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1476 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1477 | 
1478 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1479 |         return createOptionalType(
1480 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1481 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1482 | 
1483 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1484 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1485 | 
1486 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1487 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1488 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1489 | 
1490 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1491 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1492 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1493 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1494 |           builder.addAlternate(
1495 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1496 |         }
1497 |         return builder.build();
1498 | 
1499 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1500 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1501 | 
1502 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1503 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1504 | 
1505 |       case Token.STRING:
1506 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1507 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1508 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1509 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1510 |         }
1511 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1512 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1513 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1514 |           if (typeList != null &&
1515 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1516 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1517 |             JSType parameterType =
1518 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1519 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1520 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1521 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1522 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1523 |               JSType indexType =
1524 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1525 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1527 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1528 |             }
1529 |           }
1530 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1531 |         } else {
1532 |           return namedType;
1533 |         }
1534 | 
1535 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1536 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1537 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1538 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1539 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1540 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1541 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1542 |           thisType =
1543 |               ObjectType.cast(
1544 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1545 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1546 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1547 |           if (thisType == null) {
1548 |             reporter.warning(
1549 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1550 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1551 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1552 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1553 |                 sourceName,
1554 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1555 |           }
1556 | 
1557 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1558 |           current = current.getNext();
1559 |         }
1560 | 
1561 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1562 | 
1563 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1564 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1565 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1566 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1567 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1568 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1569 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1570 |               } else {
1571 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1572 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1573 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1574 |               }
1575 |             } else {
1576 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1577 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1578 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1579 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1580 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1581 |                   reporter.warning(
1582 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1583 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1584 |                 }
1585 |               } else {
1586 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1587 |               }
1588 |             }
1589 |           }
1590 |           current = current.getNext();
1591 |         }
1592 | 
1593 |         JSType returnType =
1594 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1595 | 
1596 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1597 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1598 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1599 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1600 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1601 |             .build();
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1605 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1606 |   }

95 |   @Override
96 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType other) {
97 |     if (!other.isRecordType()) {
98 |       return false;
99 |     }
100 | 
101 |     // Compare properties.
102 |     RecordType otherRecord = other.toMaybeRecordType();
103 |     if (otherRecord == this) {
104 |       return true;
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     Set<String> keySet = properties.keySet();
108 |     Map<String, JSType> otherProps = otherRecord.properties;
109 |     if (!otherProps.keySet().equals(keySet)) {
110 |       return false;
111 |     }
112 |     for (String key : keySet) {
113 |       if (!otherProps.get(key).isEquivalentTo(properties.get(key))) {
114 |         return false;
115 |       }
116 |     }
117 |     return true;
118 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
466 |     @Override
467 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
468 |         Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
470 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
471 |           n.isScript()) {
472 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
473 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
477 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
478 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
480 | 
481 |       if (descend) {
482 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
483 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
484 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
485 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();

493 |       }

===== M0746 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1849-1873
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1849 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1850 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1851 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
1852 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1853 |             type.isUnknownType());
1854 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1855 |     if (info != null) {
1856 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1857 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1858 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1859 |         type = infoType;
1860 |       }
1861 | 
1862 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1863 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
1864 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1865 |         compiler.report(
1866 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1867 |       }
1868 |     }
1869 | 
1870 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1871 |       n.setJSType(type);
1872 |     }
1873 |   }

450 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
451 |       List<JSModule> modules) {

===== M0762 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0784 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1156-1178
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1156 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1157 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1158 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1159 | 
1160 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1161 |     // and break the cycle.
1162 |     while (true) {
1163 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1164 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1165 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1166 |         return false;
1167 |       }
1168 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1169 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1170 |         return true;
1171 |       }
1172 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1173 |       if (ctor == null) {
1174 |         return false;
1175 |       }
1176 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1177 |     }
1178 |   }

===== M0797 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseAdd(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 707-739
Documentation:

Method code:
707 |   private FlowScope traverseAdd(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
708 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
709 |     Node right = left.getNext();
710 |     scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
711 | 
712 |     JSType leftType = left.getJSType();
713 |     JSType rightType = right.getJSType();
714 | 
715 |     JSType type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
716 |     if (leftType != null && rightType != null) {
717 |       boolean leftIsUnknown = leftType.isUnknownType();
718 |       boolean rightIsUnknown = rightType.isUnknownType();
719 |       if (leftIsUnknown && rightIsUnknown) {
720 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
721 |       } else if ((!leftIsUnknown && leftType.isString()) ||
722 |                  (!rightIsUnknown && rightType.isString())) {
723 |         type = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
724 |       } else if (leftIsUnknown || rightIsUnknown) {
725 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
726 |       } else if (isAddedAsNumber(leftType) && isAddedAsNumber(rightType)) {
727 |         type = getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE);
728 |       } else {
729 |         type = registry.createUnionType(STRING_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE);
730 |       }
731 |     }
732 |     n.setJSType(type);
733 | 
734 |     if (n.isAssignAdd()) {
735 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, left, leftType, type);
736 |     }
737 | 
738 |     return scope;
739 |   }

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 511-556
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
512 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
513 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
514 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
515 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
516 |     if (objectType == null) {
517 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
518 |     } else {
519 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
520 |         return;
521 |       }
522 | 
523 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
524 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
525 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
526 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
527 |         //
528 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
529 |         //    inferred type.
530 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
531 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
532 |         //    define it.
533 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
534 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
535 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
536 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
537 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
538 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
539 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
540 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
541 |           } else {
542 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
543 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
544 |           }
545 |         } else {
546 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().isThis() &&
547 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
548 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
549 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
550 |           } else {
551 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
552 |           }
553 |         }
554 |       }
555 |     }
556 |   }

660 |     void processObjectLitProperties(
661 |         Node objLit, ObjectType objLitType,

===== M0822 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 441-453
Documentation:

Method code:
441 |     @Override
442 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
443 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
444 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
445 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
446 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
447 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
448 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }

===== M0824 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.getReadableJSTypeName(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 706-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node, get a human-readable name for the type of that node so
   * that will be easy for the programmer to find the original declaration.
   *
   * For example, if SubFoo's property "bar" might have the human-readable
   * name "Foo.prototype.bar".
   *
   * @param n The node.
   * @param dereference If true, the type of the node will be dereferenced
   *     to an Object type, if possible.
   */
Method code:
706 |   String getReadableJSTypeName(Node n, boolean dereference) {
707 |     // If we're analyzing a GETPROP, the property may be inherited by the
708 |     // prototype chain. So climb the prototype chain and find out where
709 |     // the property was originally defined.
710 |     if (n.isGetProp()) {
711 |       ObjectType objectType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild()).dereference();
712 |       if (objectType != null) {
713 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
714 |         if (objectType.getConstructor() != null &&
715 |             objectType.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
716 |           objectType = FunctionType.getTopDefiningInterface(
717 |               objectType, propName);
718 |         } else {
719 |           // classes
720 |           while (objectType != null && !objectType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
721 |             objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
722 |           }
723 |         }
724 | 
725 |         // Don't show complex function names or anonymous types.
726 |         // Instead, try to get a human-readable type name.
727 |         if (objectType != null &&
728 |             (objectType.getConstructor() != null ||
729 |              objectType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
730 |           return objectType.toString() + "." + propName;
731 |         }
732 |       }
733 |     }
734 | 
735 |     JSType type = getJSType(n);
736 |     if (dereference) {
737 |       ObjectType dereferenced = type.dereference();
738 |       if (dereferenced != null) {
739 |         type = dereferenced;
740 |       }
741 |     }
742 | 
743 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
744 |     if (type.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
745 |         (type.toObjectType() != null &&
746 |          type.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null)) {
747 |       return type.toString();
748 |     } else if (qualifiedName != null) {
749 |       return qualifiedName;
750 |     } else if (type.isFunctionType()) {
751 |       // Don't show complex function names.
752 |       return "function";
753 |     } else {
754 |       return type.toString();
755 |     }
756 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 844-945
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
844 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
845 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
846 |         @Nullable String name,
847 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
848 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
849 | 
850 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
851 | 
852 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
853 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
854 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
855 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
856 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
857 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
858 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
859 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
860 |             functionType = aliasedType;
861 | 
862 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
863 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
864 |             }
865 |           }
866 |         }
867 |       }
868 | 
869 |       if (functionType == null) {
870 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
871 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
872 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
873 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
874 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
875 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
876 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
877 | 
878 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
879 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
880 | 
881 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
882 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
883 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
884 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
885 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
886 |           }
887 |         }
888 | 
889 |         if (functionType == null) {
890 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
891 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
892 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
893 |           Var ownerVar = null;
894 |           String propName = null;
895 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
896 |           if (ownerName != null) {
897 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
898 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
899 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
900 |             }
901 |             if (name != null) {
902 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
903 |             }
904 |           }
905 | 
906 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
907 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
908 |             overriddenPropType =
909 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
910 |           }
911 | 
912 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
913 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
914 |                   scope)
915 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
916 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
917 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
918 |               .inferReturnType(info)
919 |               .inferInheritance(info);
920 | 
921 | 
922 |           // Infer the context type.
923 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
924 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
925 |             builder.inferThisType(
926 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
927 |             searchedForThisType = true;
928 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
929 |             builder.inferThisType(info, ownerNode.getJSType());
930 |             searchedForThisType = true;
931 |           }
932 | 
933 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
934 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
935 |           }
936 | 
937 |           functionType = builder
938 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
939 |               .buildAndRegister();
940 |         }
941 |       }
942 | 
943 |       // all done
944 |       return functionType;
945 |     }

249 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
250 |     switch (n.getType()) {

292 |         break;

===== M0835 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 74-129
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
74 |   @Override
75 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
76 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
77 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
78 |     if (type != null) {
79 |       Node subclass = null;
80 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
81 | 
82 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
83 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
84 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
85 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
86 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
87 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
88 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
89 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
90 |           callName.isGetProp();
91 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
92 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
93 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
94 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
95 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
96 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
97 |       } else {
98 |         return null;
99 |       }
100 | 
101 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
102 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
103 |         // inheritance.
104 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
105 |           return null;
106 |         }
107 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
108 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
109 |             return null;
110 |           }
111 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
112 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
113 |         }
114 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
115 |       }
116 | 
117 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
118 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
119 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
120 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
121 |       if (subclass != null &&
122 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
123 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
124 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
125 |       }
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     return null;
129 |   }

/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
199 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
200 |     initialize();
201 |     int step = 0;
202 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
203 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
204 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
205 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
206 |       }
207 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
208 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
209 |       joinInputs(curNode);
210 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
211 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
212 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
213 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
214 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
215 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
216 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
217 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
218 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 |       step++;
223 |     }
224 |     if (isForward()) {
225 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
226 |     }
227 |   }

===== M0854 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 828-855
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
828 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
829 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
830 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
831 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
832 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
833 |           return;
834 |         }
835 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
836 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
837 | 
838 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
839 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
840 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
841 |           } else {
842 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           }
844 |         } else { // Has a catch.
845 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
846 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
847 |             return;
848 |           } else {
849 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
850 |           }
851 |         }
852 |         lastJump = handler;
853 |       }
854 |     }
855 |   }

===== M0856 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 637-664
Documentation:

Method code:
637 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
638 |     Node lastJump = null;
639 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
640 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
641 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
642 |         break;
643 |       }
644 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
645 |         if (lastJump == null) {
646 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
647 |         } else {
648 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
649 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
650 |         }
651 |         lastJump = curHandler;
652 |       }
653 |     }
654 | 
655 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
656 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
657 |     }
658 | 
659 |     if (lastJump == null) {
660 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
661 |     } else {
662 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
663 |     }
664 |   }
